{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EqcaYjT3TFdmWb9RHLdrHu","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t#!/usr/bin/env python3\n2\t\"\"\"Fig 3: Layer-wise Probe Accuracy — restyled with unified color system.\"\"\"\n3\t\n4\timport json\n5\timport numpy as np\n6\timport matplotlib\n7\tmatplotlib.use('Agg')\n8\timport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n9\tfrom pathlib import Path\n10\t\n11\t# ── Color system ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n12\tNAVY      = '#1B2838'\n13\tSLATE     = '#3D5A80'\n14\tTEAL      = '#5BA4B5'\n15\tCORAL     = '#E07A5F'\n16\tEN_BLUE   = '#4A90D9'\n17\tZH_RED    = '#E07A5F'\n18\tCROSS_TEAL= '#5BA4B5'\n19\t\n20\tCROSS_TEAL_DARK = '#3D8A9A'\n21\tDIR_STYLE = {\n22\t    'en_en': dict(color=EN_BLUE, ls='-',  lw=1.8, label=r'EN$\\rightarrow$EN'),\n23\t    'zh_zh': dict(color=ZH_RED,  ls='-',  lw=1.8, label=r'ZH$\\rightarrow$ZH'),\n24\t    'en_zh': dict(color=CROSS_TEAL, ls=(0, (5, 3)), lw=1.3, label=r'EN$\\rightarrow$ZH'),\n25\t    'zh_en': dict(color=CROSS_TEAL_DARK, ls=(0, (3, 1, 1, 1)), lw=1.3, label=r'ZH$\\rightarrow$EN'),\n26\t}\n27\tPEAK_MARKERS = {'en_en': 'o', 'zh_zh': 's', 'en_zh': '^', 'zh_en': 'D'}\n28\tCURVE_MARKERS = {'en_en': 'o', 'zh_zh': 's', 'en_zh': '^', 'zh_en': 'D'}\n29\tCURVE_MARK_EVERY = 4\n30\tMLP_GRAY = '#C0C0C0'\n31\t\n32\t# ── Paths ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n33\tDATA_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / 'artifacts' / 'layerwise_probe_curves.json'\n34\tOUT_DIR   = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\n35\tOUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n36\t\n37\twith open(DATA_PATH) as f:\n38\t    data = json.load(f)\n39\t\n40\t# ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n41\tdef get_curve(section, direction):\n42\t    d = section['directions'][direction]\n43\t    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n44\t    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), np.array([d[l] for l in layers])\n45\t\n46\tdef get_ci(ci_section, direction):\n47\t    d = ci_section['directions'][direction]\n48\t    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n49\t    lo = np.array([d[l]['ci_lower'] for l in layers])\n50\t    hi = np.array([d[l]['ci_upper'] for l in layers])\n51\t    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), lo, hi\n52\t\n53\t# ── Global style ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n54\tplt.rcParams.update({\n55\t    'font.family': 'serif',\n56\t    'font.serif': ['Times New Roman', 'Times', 'DejaVu Serif'],\n57\t    'mathtext.fontset': 'stix',\n58\t    'axes.facecolor': '#FFFFFF',\n59\t    'figure.facecolor': '#FFFFFF',\n60\t    'axes.edgecolor': SLATE,\n61\t    'axes.linewidth': python3\n\"\"\"Fig 3: Layer-wise Probe Accuracy — restyled with unified color system.\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport matplotlib\nmatplotlib.use('Agg')\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# ── Color system ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nNAVY      = '#1B2838'\nSLATE     = '#3D5A80'\nTEAL      = '#5BA4B5'\nCORAL     = '#E07A5F'\nEN_BLUE   = '#4A90D9'\nZH_RED    = '#E07A5F'\nCROSS_TEAL= '#5BA4B5'\n\nCROSS_TEAL_DARK = '#3D8A9A'\nDIR_STYLE = {\n    'en_en': dict(color=EN_BLUE, ls='-',  lw=1.8, label=r'EN$\\rightarrow$EN'),\n    'zh_zh': dict(color=ZH_RED,  ls='-',  lw=1.8, label=r'ZH$\\rightarrow$ZH'),\n    'en_zh': dict(color=CROSS_TEAL, ls=(0, (5, 3)), lw=1.3, label=r'EN$\\rightarrow$ZH'),\n    'zh_en': dict(color=CROSS_TEAL_DARK, ls=(0, (3, 1, 1, 1)), lw=1.3, label=r'ZH$\\rightarrow$EN'),\n}\nPEAK_MARKERS = {'en_en': 'o', 'zh_zh': 's', 'en_zh': '^', 'zh_en': 'D'}\nCURVE_MARKERS = {'en_en': 'o', 'zh_zh': 's', 'en_zh': '^', 'zh_en': 'D'}\nCURVE_MARK_EVERY = 4\nMLP_GRAY = '#C0C0C0'\n\n# ── Paths ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nDATA_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / 'artifacts' / 'layerwise_probe_curves.json'\nOUT_DIR   = Path(__file__).resolve().parent\nOUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\nwith open(DATA_PATH) as f:\n    data = json.load(f)\n\n# ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\ndef get_curve(section, direction):\n    d = section['directions'][direction]\n    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), np.array([d[l] for l in layers])\n\ndef get_ci(ci_section, direction):\n    d = ci_section['directions'][direction]\n    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n    lo = np.array([d[l]['ci_lower'] for l in layers])\n    hi = np.array([d[l]['ci_upper'] for l in layers])\n    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), lo, hi\n\n# ── Global style ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nplt.rcParams.update({\n    'font.family': 'serif',\n    'font.serif': ['Times New Roman', 'Times', 'DejaVu Serif'],\n    'mathtext.fontset': 'stix',\n    'axes.facecolor': '#FFFFFF',\n    'figure.facecolor': '#FFFFFF',\n    'axes.edgecolor': SLATE,\n    'axes.linewidth': 0.8,\n    'xtick.color': SLATE,\n    'ytick.color': SLATE,\n    'xtick.major.width': 0.6,\n    'ytick.major.width': 0.6,\n    'axes.grid': False,\n})\n\n# ── Models to plot ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\nmodels = []\nif 'llama31' in data:\n    models.append(('(a) Llama-3.1-8B', 'llama31', 'llama31_mlp', None, 32))\nif 'qwen3' in data:\n    models.append(('(b) Qwen3-8B', 'qwen3', 'qwen3_mlp', 'qwen3_bootstrap_ci', 36))\nif 'gemma2' in data:\n    models.append(('(c) Gemma-2-9B', 'gemma2', None, 'gemma2_bootstrap_ci', 42))\n\nn_panels = len(models)\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, n_panels, figsize=(5.5 * n_panels, 4.2), sharey=True)\nif n_panels == 1:\n    axes = [axes]\n\nfor ax, (title, res_key, mlp_key, ci_key, n_layers) in zip(axes, models):\n    directions = ['en_en', 'zh_zh', 'en_zh', 'zh_en']\n\n    # CI bands (if available)\n    if ci_key and ci_key in data:\n        for d in directions:\n            s = DIR_STYLE[d]\n            cx, clo, chi = get_ci(data[ci_key], d)\n            ax.fill_between(cx, clo, chi, color=s['color'], alpha=0.10, zorder=0)\n\n    # MLP output (de-emphasized, light gray dashed)\n    if mlp_key and mlp_key in data:\n        for d in directions:\n            mx, my = get_curve(data[mlp_key], d)\n            ax.plot(mx, my, color=MLP_GRAY, linewidth=0.8, linestyle='--',\n                    alpha=0.5, zorder=1)\n\n    # Main residual stream curves\n    for d in directions:\n        s = DIR_STYLE[d]\n        lx, ly = get_curve(data[res_key], d)\n        ax.plot(lx, ly, color=s['color'], linewidth=s['lw'], linestyle=s['ls'],\n                marker=CURVE_MARKERS[d], markersize=3.5, markevery=CURVE_MARK_EVERY,\n                markeredgecolor='white', markeredgewidth=0.4,\n                zorder=3, alpha=0.9)\n\n    # Peak markers + vertical lines\n    for d in directions:\n        s = DIR_STYLE[d]\n        pk = data[res_key]['peaks'][d]\n        pl, pa = pk['peak_layer'], pk['peak_acc']\n        ax.plot(pl, pa, marker=PEAK_MARKERS[d], markersize=9,\n                color=s['color'], zorder=6,\n                markeredgecolor='white', markeredgewidth=1.0)\n        ax.axvline(x=pl, ymin=0, ymax=0.03, color=s['color'],\n                   linewidth=0.8, alpha=0.5, zorder=1,\n                   clip_on=False)\n\n    # Styling\n    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=12, fontweight='bold', pad=8, color=NAVY)\n    ax.set_xlabel('Layer Index', fontsize=10.5, color=SLATE)\n    ax.set_xlim(-0.5, n_layers - 0.5)\n    ax.set_ylim(0.44, 0.82)\n    ax.tick_params(labelsize=9)\n    ax.spines['top'].set_visible(False)\n    ax.spines['right'].set_visible(False)\n\n    # Chance-level reference\n    ax.axhline(y=0.5, color='#CCCCCC', linewidth=0.7, linestyle=':', zorder=0)\n\naxes[0].set_ylabel('Balanced Accuracy', fontsize=10.5, color=SLATE)\n\n# ── Legend ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nfrom matplotlib.lines import Line2D\nhandles = []\nfor d in ['en_en', 'zh_zh', 'en_zh', 'zh_en']:\n    s = DIR_STYLE[d]\n    handles.append(Line2D([], [], color=s['color'], lw=s['lw'], ls=s['ls'],\n                          marker=PEAK_MARKERS[d], markersize=6,\n                          markeredgecolor='white', markeredgewidth=0.6,\n                          label=s['label']))\nhandles.append(Line2D([], [], color=MLP_GRAY, lw=0.8, ls='--', alpha=0.5,\n                       label='MLP output'))\nhandles.append(Line2D([], [], color='#CCCCCC', lw=0.7, ls=':',\n                       label='Chance'))\n\nfig.legend(handles=handles, loc='lower center', ncol=6, fontsize=8.5,\n           frameon=False, bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, -0.06),\n           columnspacing=1.8, handletextpad=0.5)\n\nplt.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0.05, 1, 1])\n\n# ── Save ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\nfig.savefig(OUT_DIR / 'fig3_layerwise.pdf', bbox_inches='tight', dpi=300)\nfig.savefig(OUT_DIR / 'fig3_layerwise.png', bbox_inches='tight', dpi=300)\nprint(f'Saved: {OUT_DIR / \"fig3_layerwise.png\"}')\nprint(f'Saved: {OUT_DIR / \"fig3_layerwise.pdf\"}')\nplt.close()\n","numLines":161,"startLine":1,"totalLines":161}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Rnf3NSr9wnSaVNBEHkp1Qj","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"path\\\": \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-prod42/projects/clkc_mech_interp/memory.md\\\", \\\"content\\\": \\\"# 关键研究发现\\\\n\\\\n## Causal Gradient Profile — 完整 6 层同条件数据 [2026-05-24]\\\\n\\\\n**核心发现**：同条件实验（N=201, probe acc ~85-92%）绘制出完整的因果梯度。Steering 效应 peak at L13，向深层平滑衰减，L28（detection peak）完全无效。\\\\n\\\\n| Layer | Depth | Probe Acc | Best α | Fisher p | Bonferroni sig | 效应 |\\\\n|-------|-------|-----------|--------|----------|----------------|------|\\\\n| L12 | 38% | 91.75% | +20 | 0.0853 | 0/6 ❌ | — |\\\\n| **L13** | **41%** | **90.00%** | **+10** | **0.0003** | **2/6** ✅✅ | **peak** |\\\\n| **L14** | **44%** | **91.75%** | **+10** | **0.0022** | **1/6** ✅ | strong |\\\\n| **L16** | **50%** | **91.50%** | **+10** | **0.005** | **1/6** ✅ | moderate |\\\\n| L18 | 56% | 90.75% | +5 | 0.0099 | 0/6 ❌ | marginal (差0.0016) |\\\\n| L28 | 88% | 85.25% | +10 | 0.284 | 0/6 ❌ | none |\\\\n\\\\n**Causal window = L13-L16（3层显著）**，L18 marginal，L12/L28 negative。窗口占 32 层模型的 ~12.5%。\\\\n\\\\n**p 值单调递增梯度**：0.0003 → 0.0022 → 0.005 → 0.0099 → 0.284。连续衰减而非 on/off。\\\\n\\\\n**Detection ≠ Decision 最终验证**：L28 是 detection peak（probe acc 最高），但 steering best p=0.284，完全无因果效应。高质量 probe 条件下解离依然稳健。\\\\n\\\\n**仅正向 α 有效**：所有显著效应来自 +5/+10，负向 α 全不显著。\\\\n\\\\n**L13 复现更强**：原始 p=0.0009 (N=136, 1α sig) → 新数据 p=0.0003 (N=201, 2α sig)。\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## C2 不对称性非冲突特有 [2026-05-22, xnli_baseline_110]\\\\n\\\\n**核心发现**：~6pp ZH→EN > EN→ZH 不对称性在非冲突任务 XNLI 上同样存在，是模型的一般性跨语言属性，非冲突信号特有。\\\\n\\\\n| Model | XNLI asymmetry | Conflict asymmetry |\\\\n|-------|---------------|-------------------|\\\\n| Qwen | +6.72pp CI[2.36,11.73] ✓ | +5.95pp CI[-3.67,14.81] |\\\\n| Llama | +5.97pp CI[0.92,11.33] ✓ | +11.76pp CI[4.29,19.57] ✓ |\\\\n\\\\n**推导**：英语主导预训练 → EN 表征空间更\\\\\\\"标准\\\\\\\" → ZH→EN 迁移天然更好。C2 需 reframe：从 \\\\\\\"conflict-specific asymmetry\\\\\\\" 改为 \\\\\\\"conflict signals share general cross-lingual transfer pathways\\\\\\\"。\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## Steering 因果效应是 Llama-specific [2026-05-22, 更新 2026-05-24]\\\\n\\\\n**核心发现**：Probe weight 方向的因果效应具有强模型特异性——仅 Llama L13-L16 有效，Qwen 和 Gemma 全部 negative。\\\\n\\\\n### Llama 完整层扫描（同条件 N=201）\\\\n| 层 | 最佳 Fisher p | 结论 |\\\\n|----|-------------|------|\\\\n| L12 | 0.0853 | NEGATIVE |\\\\n| **L13** | **0.0003** | **SUCCESS** |\\\\n| **L14** | **0.0022** | **SUCCESS** |\\\\n| **L16** | **0.005** | **SUCCESS** |\\\\n| L18 | 0.0099 | NEGATIVE (marginal) |\\\\n| L28 | 0.284 | NEGATIVE |\\\\n\\\\n### 跨模型结果（含匹配条件重跑）\\\\n| 模型 | 层 | 深度 | 最佳 p | N | Probe Acc | 结论 |\\\\n|------|-----|------|--------|---|-----------|------|\\\\n| **Llama L13** | 41% | 0.0003 | 201 | 90.0% | **SUCCESS** |\\\\n| **Llama L14** | 44% | 0.0022 | 201 | 91.8% | **SUCCESS** |\\\\n| **Llama L16** | 50% | 0.005 | 201 | 91.5% | **SUCCESS** |\\\\n| Qwen L14 | 39% | 0.682 | 84 | 70.2% | NEGATIVE |\\\\n| Qwen L18 | 50% | 0.620 | 84 | 73.8% | NEGATIVE |\\\\n| Qwen L23 | 64% | 1.0 | 84 | 73.2% | NEGATIVE |\\\\n| Gemma L16 | 38% | 1.000 | 88 | 59.7% | NEGATIVE |\\\\n| Gemma L17 | 40% | 1.000 | 88 | 66.5% | NEGATIVE |\\\\n| Gemma L18 | 43% | 0.497 | 88 | 61.9% | NEGATIVE |\\\\n| Gemma L10 | 24% | 0.214 | 158 | 70.6% | NEGATIVE (旧条件) |\\\\n| Gemma L17 | 40% | 0.104 | 158 | 75.3% | NEGATIVE (旧条件) |\\\\n| Gemma L24 | 57% | 1.0 | 158 | 76.3% | NEGATIVE (旧条件) |\\\\n\\\\n### Gemma 匹配条件重跑分析 [2026-05-24]\\\\n- N=88（仅 88 样本通过 parametric knowledge 验证，vs Llama 201）\\\\n- Probe acc 60-66%（vs Llama 91%）——线性探针方向不足以捕获冲突信号\\\\n- Baseline 中 parametric=1/88——模型几乎不产出 parametric 回答，steering 上行空间极其有限\\\\n- L17 从旧条件 p=0.104 恶化到匹配条件 p=1.000（零 flip），说明旧实验的 marginal trend 是噪声\\\\n- **结论**：Gemma 的冲突表征可能不是线性的，或 ConflictQA 对 Gemma 的 parametric knowledge 覆盖不足\\\\n\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n## 踩坑记录\\\\n\\\\n### Probe Quality 是 Steering 效果的关键调节变量 [2026-05-24]\\\\n- L16 在低 probe acc (77%) 下 negative (p=0.12)，高 probe acc (91.5%) 下 success (p=0.005)\\\\n- **教训**：比较不同条件的 steering 结果时必须控制 probe quality。不同数据采样/训练集大小影响 probe 准确率，进而影响 steering direction 精度\\\\n\\\\n### CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 映射规则 [2026-05-22]\\\\n- SSH 手动执行时 `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES` 直接对应物理 GPU 编号\\\\n- tmux 内层 `bash -c` 会覆盖外层值\\\\n\\\\n### Steering 脚本不写 W&B → cron 误判 [2026-05-22]\\\\n- steering_experiment.py 输出到 JSON，不记录 W&B metrics → cron 误标 failed\\\\n- **处理**：收到 steering 的 failed 回调时，先读 JSON 再下结论\\\\n\\\\n### EN→EN Probe Accuracy 数据溯源 [2026-05-22]\\\\n- Llama EN→EN peak: **0.7757 (77.6%)** at layer 28\\\\n- Qwen EN→EN peak: **0.75 (75.0%)** at layer 24\\\\n\\\\n### 论文数据一致性修正 [2026-05-24]\\\\n- Appendix C L28 Best p: 0.740 是 α=-20 的 p，\\\\\\\"Best p\\\\\\\" 定义为 min across all α = 0.310，已修正\\\\n- artifacts experiments.tex L28 (all) p: 0.370 数学上不可能（equal flip rate → p=1.000），已修正为 1.000\\\\n- L18 \\\\\\\"non-significance\\\\\\\" 需加 \\\\\\\"after Bonferroni correction\\\\\\\" 限定（p=0.010 名义上显著），已修正\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 4167, \\\"truncated\\\": false}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01Dwa24tPrrQvT1foMQ81mxn","session_id":"f77b7cc6-c741-4b5f-bdca-bc51f0314a45","uuid":"cabc4ed1-3f2c-41c5-af96-893c1d8b9523","timestamp":"2026-05-26T08:06:23.910Z"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01767uESsn9Mropp1buNWLnR","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t{\n2\t  \"raw\": \"任务完成。汇报如下：\\n\\n**Gemma 数据**\\n- 存在于 `/root/clkc_mech_interp/data/gemma2/probe_results_bootstrap_n2000.json`\\n- CLKC conflict 数据，residual stream probe，42 层（layer 0-41），4 方向（en_en/zh_zh/en_zh/zh_en）\\n- bootstrap n=2000，含 mean/ci_lower/ci_upper\\n- 无 MLP probe 数据（Llama/Qwen 有 MLP，Gemma 面板仅显示 residual stream + CI band）\\n\\n**脚本修改**\\n1. `aggregate_layerwise.py`：追加了 Gemma2 数据提取，将 bootstrap mean 作为 residual stream 值，CI 数据单独存储为 `gemma2_bootstrap_ci`\\n2. `gen_fig2_layerwise_probes.py`：\\n   - `1×2` → `1×3` 布局，figsize `(7.0, 3.4)` → `(10.5, 3.4)`\\n   - 添加 `(c) Gemma-2-9B` 面板（42 层，residual stream + CI band，无 MLP）\\n   - MLP 绘制逻辑改为可选（`mlp_key` 为 None 时跳过）\\n\\n**Gemma 峰值**\\n| Direction | Peak Layer | Accuracy |\\n|-----------|-----------|----------|\\n| en_en | L24 | 0.7617 |\\n| zh_zh | L24 | 0.7097 |\\n| en_zh | L22 | 0.6290 |\\n| zh_en | L24 | 0.7023 |\\n\\n**生成文件**\\n- `/root/clkc_mech_interp/figures/paper/fig2_layerwise_probes.pdf` (40KB)\\n- `/root/clkc_mech_interp/figures/paper/fig2_layerwise_probes.png` (775KB)\\n- 图片尺寸 3119×1120，三面板均正确渲染\\n\\n**视觉验证**：无法通过 SSH 传输大图到本地查看（binary 传输受限）。建议你直接在服务器上查看 PNG，或下载 PDF 到本地确认。\"\n3\t}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01Dwa24tPrrQvT1foMQ81mxn","session_id":"f77b7cc6-c741-4b5f-bdca-bc51f0314a45","uuid":"af7d9c85-f943-4d90-a505-e20f79602a0c","timestamp":"2026-05-26T08:07:16.128Z"}

r\"ZH$\\rightarrow$ZH\",\n22\t    \"en_zh\": r\"EN$\\rightarrow$ZH\",\n23\t    \"zh_en\": r\"ZH$\\rightarrow$EN\",\n24\t}\n25\tDIR_COLORS = {\n26\t    \"en_en\": \"#2171b5\",\n27\t    \"zh_zh\": sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n35\t    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), np.array([d[l] for l in layers])\n36\t\n37\tdef get_ci(ci_section, direction):\n38\t    d = ci_section[\"directions\"][direction]\n39\t    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n40\t    mean = np.array([d[l][\"mean\"] for l in layers])\n41\t    lo = np.array([d[l][\"ci_lower\"] for l in layers])\n42\t    hi = np.array([d[l][\"ci_upper\"] for l in layers])\n43\t    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), mean, lo, hi\n44\t\n45\t# --- figure: 1×3 layout ---\n46\tfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(10.5, 3.4), sharey=True)\n47\t\n48\tmodels = [\n49\t    (\"(a) Llama-3.1-8B\", \"llama31\", \"llama31_mlp\", None, 32),\n50\t    (\"(b) Qwen3-8B\", \"qwen3\", np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), np.array([d[l] for l in layers])\n\ndef get_ci(ci_section, direction):\n    d = ci_section[\"directions\"][direction]\n    layers = sorted(d.keys(), key=int)\n    mean = np.array([d[l][\"mean\"] for l in layers])\n    lo = np.array([d[l][\"ci_lower\"] for l in layers])\n    hi = np.array([d[l][\"ci_upper\"] for l in layers])\n    return np.array([int(l) for l in layers]), mean, lo, hi\n\n# --- figure: 1×3 layout ---\nfig, axes = plt.subplots(1, 3, figsize=(10.5, 3.4), sharey=True)\n\nmodels = [\n    (\"(a) Llama-3.1-8B\", \"llama31\", \"llama31_mlp\", None, 32),\n    (\"(b) Qwen3-8B\", \"qwen3\", \"qwen3_mlp\", \"qwen3_bootstrap_ci\", 36),\n    (\"(c) Gemma-2-9B\", \"gemma2\", None, \"gemma2_bootstrap_ci\", 42),\n]\n\nfor ax, (title, res_key, mlp_key, ci_key, n_layers) in zip(axes, models):\n    for direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n        c = DIR_COLORS[direction]\n\n        lx, ly = get_curve(data[res_key], direction)\n        ax.plot(lx, ly, color=c, linewidth=1.4, zorder=3)\n\n        if mlp_key and mlp_key in data:\n            mx, my = get_curve(data[mlp_key], direction)\n            ax.plot(mx, my, color=c, linewidth=1.0, linestyle=\"--\", alpha=0.65,\n                    zorder=2)\n\n        if ci_key and ci_key in data:\n            cx, cm, clo, chi = get_ci(data[ci_key], direction)\n            ax.fill_between(cx, clo, chi, color=c, alpha=0.08, zorder=1)\n\n    # peak markers (residual — star)\n    for direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n        pk = data[res_key][\"peaks\"][direction]\n        ax.plot(pk[\"peak_layer\"], pk[\"peak_acc\"], marker=\"*\", markersize=8,\n                color=DIR_COLORS[direction], zorder=5,\n                markeredgecolor=\"k\", markeredgewidth=0.4)\n\n    # peak markers (MLP — diamond)\n    if mlp_key and mlp_key in data:\n        for direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n            pk = data[mlp_key][\"peaks\"][direction]\n            ax.plot(pk[\"peak_layer\"], pk[\"peak_acc\"], marker=\"D\", markersize=4.5,\n                    color=DIR_COLORS[direction], zorder=5, alpha=0.85,\n                    markeredgecolor=\"k\", markeredgewidth=0.4)\n\n    # L13 annotation for Llama (steering causal node)\n    if res_key == \"llama31\":\n        ax.axvline(x=13, color=\"#555555\", linewidth=0.9, linestyle=\":\",\n                   zorder=1, alpha=0.7)\n        ax.annotate(\"L13\\n(steering)\", xy=(13, 0.47), fontsize=7.5,\n                    color=\"#555555\", ha=\"center\", va=\"bottom\",\n                    fontstyle=\"italic\")\n\n    ax.set_title(title, fontsize=11, fontweight=\"bold\", pad=6)\n    ax.set_xlabel(\"Layer Index\", fontsize=10)\n    ax.set_xlim(-0.5, n_layers - 0.5)\n    ax.set_ylim(0.45, 0.84)\n    ax.tick_params(labelsize=9)\n    ax.spines[\"top\"].set_visible(False)\n    ax.spines[\"right\"].set_visible(False)\n\naxes[0].set_ylabel(\"Balanced Accuracy\", fontsize=10)\n\n# compact legend\nhandles = []\nfor direction in [\"en_en\", \"zh_zh\", \"en_zh\", \"zh_en\"]:\n    handles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], color=DIR_COLORS[direction], lw=1.5,\n                               label=DIR_LABELS[direction]))\nhandles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], color=\"grey\", lw=1.3, ls=\"-\",\n                           label=\"Residual stream\"))\nhandles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], color=\"grey\", lw=1.0, ls=\"--\", alpha=0.65,\n                           label=\"MLP output\"))\nhandles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], marker=\"*\", color=\"grey\", ls=\"\",\n                           markersize=7, markeredgecolor=\"k\",\n                           markeredgewidth=0.3, label=\"Peak (res.)\"))\nhandles.append(plt.Line2D([], [], marker=\"D\", color=\"grey\", ls=\"\",\n                           markersize=4, markeredgecolor=\"k\",\n                           markeredgewidth=0.3, label=\"Peak (MLP)\"))\nlabels = [h.get_label() for h in handles]\n\nfig.legend(handles, labels, loc=\"lower center\", ncol=4, fontsize=7.5,\n           frameon=False, bbox_to_anchor=(0.5, -0.10), columnspacing=1.5,\n           handletextpad=0.5)\n\nplt.tight_layout(rect=[0, 0.04, 1, 1])\n\n# --- save ---\nfig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"fig2_layerwise_probes.pdf\",\n            bbox_inches=\"tight\", dpi=300)\nfig.savefig(OUT_DIR / \"fig2_layerwise_probes.png\",\n            bbox_inches=\"tight\", dpi=300)\nprint(f\"Saved to {OUT_DIR / 'fig2_layerwise_probes.pdf'}\")\nprint(f\"Saved to {OUT_DIR / 'fig2_layerwise_probes.png'}\")\nplt.close()\n","numLines":134,"startLine":1,"totalLines":134}}}
